+ +import { mkdtempSync, rmSync } from "node:fs";
+ +import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
+ +import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
+ +import { join, relative, sep } from "node:path";
+ +import { writeFileAtomic } from "../core/fsutil.ts";
+ +import { inputHash, type InputSnapshot } from "../core/input-snapshot.ts";
+ +import { safeTeamPath } from "../team/store.ts";
+ +import { fields, HandoffError, strictJson } from "./handoff-files.ts";
+ +import { metadataJson } from "./team-input.ts";
+ +import { hasTeamContract } from "./team-contract.ts";
+ +import { eventLines } from "./event-log.ts";
+ +
+ +export const teamFinishFile = "TEAM-FINISH.json";
+ +type Git = (args: string[], env?: Record<string, string>) => { status: number | null; stdout: string; stderr: string };
+ +const oid = (value: unknown): value is string => typeof value === "string" && /^(?:[a-f0-9]{40}|[a-f0-9]{64})$/.test(value);
+ +function seal(value: unknown, id: string): string {
+ +  fields(value, ["version", "kind", "project", "tree"]);
+ +  if (value.version !== 1 || value.kind !== "pace-team-finish" || value.project !== id || !oid(value.tree)) throw new HandoffError("팀 finish 영수증 형식/소유 프로젝트 오류");
+ +  return value.tree;
+ +}
+ +function run(git: Git, args: string[], env: Record<string, string> = {}): string {
+ +  const r = git(args, { GIT_NO_LAZY_FETCH: "1", ...env });
+ +  if (r.status !== 0) throw new HandoffError("팀 finish Git tree 대조 실패");
+ +  return r.stdout.trim();
+ +}
+ +function blob(git: Git, target: string, path: string): string {
+ +  const result = git(["show", `${target}:${path}`], { GIT_NO_LAZY_FETCH: "1" });
+ +  if (result.status !== 0) throw new HandoffError("팀 finish Git 입력을 읽지 못했다");
+ +  return result.stdout;
+ +}
+ +/** A private index removes the self-referential receipt without touching the user's index. */
+ +function withoutSeal(git: Git, tree: string, path: string): string {
+ +  const temp = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), "pace-team-finish-"));
+ +  try {
+ +    const env = { GIT_INDEX_FILE: join(temp, "index") };
+ +    run(git, ["read-tree", tree], env);
+ +    run(git, ["--literal-pathspecs", "update-index", "--force-remove", "--", path], env);
+ +    return run(git, ["write-tree"], env);
+ +  } finally { rmSync(temp, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
+ +}
+ +export function assertTeamFinishDestination(root: string, project: string, id: string): void {
+ +  const rel = `${project}/${teamFinishFile}`, path = safeTeamPath(root, rel);
+ +  // Existing foreign files are never adopted or overwritten.
+ +  try { seal(metadataJson(path, 4096), id); } catch (error) {
+ +    if ((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code !== "ENOENT") throw error;
+ +  }
+ +}
+ +/** Every file actually used by the team validation must survive in the committed tree. */
+ +export function assertTeamFinishInputs(root: string, inputs: InputSnapshot, git: Git, tree: string): void {
+ +  const rel = (path: string) => relative(root, path).split(sep).join("/");
+ +  const paths = new Map([...inputs.files].map(([path, hash]) => [rel(path), hash]));
+ +  for (const path of paths.keys()) safeTeamPath(root, path);
+ +  const entries = run(git, ["--literal-pathspecs", "ls-tree", "-r", "-z", tree, "--", ...paths.keys()]).split("\0");
+ +  const tracked = new Map(entries.flatMap((entry): [string, string][] => { const m = /^100(?:644|755) blob ([a-f0-9]+)\t(.+)$/s.exec(entry); return m ? [[m[2]!, m[1]!]] : []; }));
+ +  for (const [path, hash] of paths) {
+ +    if (!tracked.has(path)) throw new HandoffError(`팀 검증 자료가 Git 인덱스에 없다: ${path} — ignored/미추적 자료를 확인하고 커밋 후 finish한다`);
+ +    const staged = git(["show", `${tree}:${path}`], { GIT_NO_LAZY_FETCH: "1" });
+ +    const oid = tracked.get(path)!;
+ +    const bytes = Buffer.from(staged.stdout), decodedOid = createHash(oid.length === 40 ? "sha1" : "sha256").update(`blob ${bytes.length}\0`).update(bytes).digest("hex");
+ +    if (staged.status !== 0 || decodedOid !== oid || hash !== inputHash(bytes)) throw new HandoffError(`검증한 입력과 고정 Git tree가 다르다: ${path}`);
+ +  }
+ +  for (const path of inputs.missing) {
+ +    const entry = run(git, ["--literal-pathspecs", "ls-tree", "-z", tree, "--", rel(path)]);
+ +    if (entry) throw new HandoffError(`검증 때 없던 입력이 고정 Git tree에 생겼다: ${rel(path)}`);
+ +  }
+ +  for (const item of inputs.directories) {
+ +    const entry = run(git, ["--literal-pathspecs", "ls-tree", "-z", tree, "--", rel(item.path)]);
+ +    const directory = /^040000 tree ([a-f0-9]+)\t/.exec(entry)?.[1];
+ +    const records = directory ? run(git, ["ls-tree", ...(item.mode === "regular-files" ? ["-r"] : []), "-z", directory]).split("\0").filter(Boolean) : [];
+ +    const names = records.filter((record) => item.mode !== "regular-files" || /^100(?:644|755) blob /.test(record)).map((record) => record.slice(record.indexOf("\t") + 1)).filter(item.accepts).sort();
+ +    if (JSON.stringify(names) !== JSON.stringify(item.names)) throw new HandoffError(`검증 입력 목록과 고정 Git tree가 다르다: ${rel(item.path)}`);
+ +  }
+ +}
+ +export function writeTeamFinish(root: string, project: string, id: string, git: Git, capturedTree?: string): void {
+ +  assertTeamFinishDestination(root, project, id);
+ +  const rel = `${project}/${teamFinishFile}`, path = safeTeamPath(root, rel);
+ +  const tree = withoutSeal(git, capturedTree ?? run(git, ["write-tree"]), rel);
+ +  writeFileAtomic(path, `${JSON.stringify({ version: 1, kind: "pace-team-finish", project: id, tree })}\n`);
+ +  run(git, ["add", "--", rel]);
+ +}
+ +function sealEntry(git: Git, target: string, path: string): string | undefined {
+ +  const entry = run(git, ["--literal-pathspecs", "ls-tree", "-z", target, "--", path]);
+ +  if (!/^100(?:644|755) blob [a-f0-9]+\t/.test(entry)) return undefined;
+ +  const size = Number(run(git, ["cat-file", "-s", `${target}:${path}`]));
+ +  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(size) || size > 4096) return undefined;
+ +  return blob(git, target, path);
+ +}
+ +
+ +function changedHistory(git: Git, target: string, path: string): string[] {
+ +  const history = run(git, ["--literal-pathspecs", "log", "--full-history", "--format=%H", target, "--", path]);
+ +  const commits = history.split("\n").filter(Boolean);
+ +  if (!commits.every(oid)) throw new HandoffError("팀 finish Git 이력 대조 실패");
+ +  return commits;
+ +}
+ +
+ +export function hasTeamFinishHistory(project: string, id: string, target: string, git: Git): boolean {
+ +  const path = `${project}/${teamFinishFile}`;
+ +  for (const commit of changedHistory(git, target, path)) {
+ +    const content = sealEntry(git, commit, path);
+ +    if (content === undefined) continue;
+ +    let expected: string;
+ +    try { expected = seal(strictJson(content), id); } catch { continue; }
+ +    if (withoutSeal(git, `${commit}^{tree}`, path) === expected) return true;
+ +  }
+ +
+ +  // Current files may have been removed during rebase before a seal could be written.
+ +  const measurement = `${project}/TEAM-MEASUREMENT.json`;
+ +  for (const commit of changedHistory(git, target, measurement)) {
+ +    if (run(git, ["--literal-pathspecs", "ls-tree", "-z", commit, "--", measurement])) return true;
+ +  }
+ +  // Marker meaning can change when a surrounding fence/comment changes instead of the marker line.
+ +  for (const [name, adopted] of [
+ +    ["PROJECT.md", (text: string) => hasTeamContract(text, false, [])],
+ +    ["log.jsonl", (text: string) => hasTeamContract("", false, eventLines(text).filter((event) => event.p === id))],
+ +  ] as const) {
+ +    const source = `${project}/${name}`, checked = new Set<string>();
+ +    for (const commit of changedHistory(git, target, source)) {
+ +      const entry = run(git, ["--literal-pathspecs", "ls-tree", "-z", commit, "--", source]);
+ +      const oid = /^100(?:644|755) blob ([a-f0-9]+)\t/.exec(entry)?.[1];
+ +      if (!oid || checked.has(oid)) continue;
+ +      checked.add(oid);
+ +      if (adopted(blob(git, commit, source))) return true;
+ +    }
+ +  }
+ +  return false;
+ +}
+ +
+ +/** Returns the captured commit for adopted projects, also when their worktree is absent. */
+ +export function checkedTeamTarget(project: string, id: string, target: string, git: Git): string | undefined {
+ +  const projectText = blob(git, target, `${project}/PROJECT.md`);
+ +  const listed = run(git, ["--literal-pathspecs", "ls-tree", "-r", "--name-only", "-z", target, "--", project]).split("\0");
+ +  const events = listed.includes(`${project}/log.jsonl`) ? eventLines(blob(git, target, `${project}/log.jsonl`)) : [];
+ +  const adopted = hasTeamContract(projectText, listed.includes(`${project}/TEAM-MEASUREMENT.json`), events);
+ +  const rel = `${project}/${teamFinishFile}`;
+ +  const current = listed.includes(rel) ? sealEntry(git, target, rel) : undefined;
+ +  let expected: string | undefined;
+ +  if (current !== undefined) {
+ +    try { expected = seal(strictJson(current), id); }
+ +    catch (error) { if (adopted) throw error; }
+ +  }
+ +  if (expected === undefined) {
+ +    if (!adopted && !hasTeamFinishHistory(project, id, target, git)) return undefined;
+ +    throw new HandoffError("팀 finish 영수증이 없거나 유효하지 않다: 팀 측정 입력을 확인하고 worktree에서 finish를 다시 실행한다");
+ +  }
+ +  if (withoutSeal(git, `${target}^{tree}`, rel) !== expected) throw new HandoffError("팀 finish 뒤 내용이 바뀌었다: worktree에서 finish를 다시 실행한다");
+ +  return target;
+ +}

+ +export function comparisonContext(value: unknown): ComparisonContext {

+ +export function teamPeriod(value: unknown): TeamPeriod {

+ +  return value as unknown as TeamPeriod;

+ +export function readTeamInput(root: string, projectDir: string, inputs?: InputTrace): TeamInput | null {
+ +  const path = safeTeamPath(root, `${projectDir}/${measurementFile}`); if (!existsSync(path)) { inputs?.absent?.(path); return null; }

+ +  if (value.version !== 1) throw new HandoffError("팀 측정 입력 버전 미지원");
+ +  if (value.comparison !== undefined) comparisonContext(value.comparison);
+ +  if (value.period !== undefined) { const p = teamPeriod(value.period); if (p.completionRef) evidenceReceipt(dir, p.completionRef, inputs); }

+ +  }

+ +  if (value.request_coverage !== undefined) coverage(dir, value.request_coverage, inputs);
+ +  if (value.log_coverage !== undefined) coverage(dir, value.log_coverage, inputs);
+ +  if (value.quality_audit !== undefined) {
+ +    fields(value.quality_audit, ["blocking_sum", "rereviews_after_fail", "coverage"]);

+ +  }

+ +      fields(r, ["role", "workMs", "outputTokens", "coverage"]);

+ +import { HandoffError } from "./handoff-files.ts";
+ +
+ +const opaque = "<!-- opaque block -->";
+ +const blank = (line: string) => /^[ \t]*$/.test(line);
+ +const rawEnd = /<\/(?:pre|script|style|textarea)>/i;
+ +// CommonMark 0.31.2 §4.6: block starts differ from complete inline tags.
+ +const blockTags = new Set("address article aside base basefont blockquote body caption center col colgroup dd details dialog dir div dl dt fieldset figcaption figure footer form frame frameset h1 h2 h3 h4 h5 h6 head header hr html iframe legend li link main menu menuitem nav noframes ol optgroup option p param search section summary table tbody td tfoot th thead title tr track ul".split(" "));
+ +const rawTags = new Set(["pre", "script", "style", "textarea"]);
+ +const attribute = String.raw`(?:[ \t]+[A-Za-z_:][A-Za-z0-9_.:-]*(?:[ \t]*=[ \t]*(?:"[^"]*"|'[^']*'|[^\s"'=<>\x60]+))?)*`;
+ +const openTag = new RegExp(String.raw`^ {0,3}<([A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9-]*)${attribute}[ \t]*/?>[ \t]*$`);
+ +const closeTag = /^ {0,3}<\/([A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9-]*)[ \t]*>[ \t]*$/;
+ +function htmlStart(line: string, blockReady: boolean, cautious: boolean): { kind: string; uncertain: boolean } | null {
+ +  if (/^ {0,3}<(?:pre|script|style|textarea)(?=[ \t>]|$)/i.test(line)) return { kind: "raw", uncertain: false };
+ +  if (/^ {0,3}<!--/.test(line)) return { kind: "comment", uncertain: false };
+ +  if (/^ {0,3}<\?/.test(line)) return { kind: "pi", uncertain: false };
+ +  if (/^ {0,3}<![A-Za-z]/.test(line)) return { kind: "declaration", uncertain: false };
+ +  if (/^ {0,3}<!\[CDATA\[/.test(line)) return { kind: "cdata", uncertain: false };
+ +  const prefix = /^ {0,3}<\/?([A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9-]*)(?=[ \t>]|\/>|$)/.exec(line);
+ +  if (prefix && blockTags.has(prefix[1]!.toLowerCase())) return { kind: "blank", uncertain: false };
+ +  const opened = openTag.exec(line), closed = closeTag.exec(line);
+ +  if ((closed || opened && !rawTags.has(opened[1]!.toLowerCase())) && (blockReady || cautious)) return { kind: "blank", uncertain: cautious };
+ +  return null;
+ +}
+ +function htmlEnded(kind: string, line: string): boolean {
+ +  return kind === "blank" ? blank(line) : kind === "raw" ? rawEnd.test(line) : kind === "comment" ? line.includes("-->") : kind === "pi" ? line.includes("?>") : kind === "cdata" ? line.includes("]]>") : line.includes(">");
+ +}
+ +const thematic = /^ {0,3}(?:(?:-[ \t]*){3,}|(?:\*[ \t]*){3,}|(?:_[ \t]*){3,})$/;
+ +const atx = /^ {0,3}#{1,6}(?:[ \t]|$)/;
+ +/** Projection for canonical metadata/ATX tables; uncertain standalone HTML ends a retrospective interval. */
+ +export function teamMarkdown(text: string, atxOnly = false): string {
+ +  let fence: { char: string; length: number } | undefined, html: string | undefined, comment = false, indentedCode = false, blockReady = true, pipeBlock = false;
+ +  const inline = (line: string): string => {
+ +    let out = "", at = 0;
+ +    while (at < line.length) {
+ +      if (comment) {
+ +        const end = line.indexOf("-->", at);
+ +        if (end < 0) return out;
+ +        at = end + 3; comment = false; out += " "; continue;
+ +      }
+ +      if (line[at] === "\\" && at + 1 < line.length) { out += line.slice(at, at + 2); at += 2; continue; }
+ +      if (/^<\/?[a-z][a-z0-9-]*(?:\s|\/?>)/i.test(line.slice(at))) {
+ +        let end = at + 1, quote = "";
+ +        for (; end < line.length; end++) { const char = line[end]!; if (quote) { if (char === quote) quote = ""; } else if (char === "\"" || char === "'") quote = char; else if (char === ">") { end++; break; } }
+ +        out += line.slice(at, end); at = end; continue;
+ +      }
+ +      if (line[at] === "`") {
+ +        const run = /^`+/.exec(line.slice(at))![0]; let end = at + run.length;
+ +        while ((end = line.indexOf(run, end)) >= 0) { if (line[end - 1] !== "`" && line[end + run.length] !== "`") break; end += run.length; }
+ +        if (end >= 0) { out += "x".repeat(end + run.length - at); at = end + run.length; continue; }
+ +        if (line.slice(at + run.length).includes("<!--")) throw new HandoffError("팀 Markdown의 여러 줄/미완성 코드와 주석 혼합은 일반 텍스트로 정리한다");
+ +        out += run; at += run.length; continue;
+ +      }
+ +      if (line.startsWith("<!--", at)) { comment = true; at += 4; out += " "; continue; }
+ +      out += line[at++]!;
+ +    }
+ +    return out;
+ +  };
+ +  return text.replace(/\r\n?/g, "\n").split("\n").map((line, index, lines) => {
+ +    const priorBlockReady = blockReady; blockReady = blank(line);
+ +    if (!line.startsWith("|")) pipeBlock = false;
+ +    if (fence) {
+ +      const close = /^ {0,3}([`~]+)[ \t]*$/.exec(line);
+ +      if (close && close[1]!.length >= fence.length && [...close[1]!].every((char) => char === fence!.char)) { fence = undefined; blockReady = true; }
+ +      return opaque;
+ +    }
+ +    if (html) { if (htmlEnded(html, line)) { html = undefined; blockReady = true; } return opaque; }
+ +    // Inline comments cannot escape their paragraph into a new block. Block comments use html above.
+ +    const interrupt = blank(line) || atx.test(line) || thematic.test(line) || /^ {0,3}(?:`{3,}|~{3,}|>|[-+*][ \t]+\S|1[.)][ \t]+\S)/.test(line) || !!htmlStart(line, false, false);
+ +    if (comment && interrupt) comment = false;
+ +    let indentation = 0; for (const char of line) { if (char === " ") indentation++; else if (char === "\t") indentation += 4 - indentation % 4; else break; }
+ +    if (!comment && indentation >= 4 && (priorBlockReady || indentedCode)) { indentedCode = true; blockReady = true; return opaque; }
+ +    if (!comment && !blank(line)) indentedCode = false;
+ +    if (!comment && /^ {1,3}#{1,6}(?:[ \t]|$)/.test(line)) return "<!-- unsupported indented heading -->";
+ +    if (!comment) {
+ +      const start = htmlStart(line, priorBlockReady, atxOnly);
+ +      if (start) { html = htmlEnded(start.kind, line) ? undefined : start.kind; if (!html) blockReady = true; return start.uncertain ? "## opaque HTML boundary" : opaque; }
+ +      const open = /^ {0,3}(`{3,}|~{3,})/.exec(line);
+ +      if (open && open[1]![0] === "`" && line.slice(open[0].length).includes("`")) throw new HandoffError("팀 Markdown의 backtick fence 정보는 backtick을 포함할 수 없다");
+ +      if (open) { fence = { char: open[1]![0]!, length: open[1]!.length }; return opaque; }
+ +    }
+ +    const continuedInlineComment = comment, visible = inline(line);
+ +    if (continuedInlineComment) return opaque;
+ +    if (comment) return line;
+ +    // Removing inline HTML must never invent a block marker absent in the source.
+ +    if (atx.test(visible) && !atx.test(line)) return line;
+ +    if (atx.test(visible)) blockReady = true;
+ +    const separator = /^ {0,3}(?:=+|(?:-[ \t]*)+|(?:\*[ \t]*)+|(?:_[ \t]*)+)$/.test(visible);
+ +    if (thematic.test(visible)) blockReady = true;
+ +    if (atxOnly && separator) return "<!-- unsupported underline -->";
+ +    if (/^ {0,3}(?:=+|-+)[ \t]*$/.test(visible)) { const prior = lines[index - 1]?.trim() ?? ""; return prior && !/^#{1,6}(?:[ \t]|$)/.test(prior) && !/^(?:[<>]|`{3}|~{3})/.test(prior) ? "## opaque boundary" : "<!-- opaque boundary -->"; }
+ +    if (atxOnly && line.startsWith("|")) {
+ +      if (!pipeBlock && !priorBlockReady) return opaque;
+ +      pipeBlock = true;
+ +      return line;
+ +    }
+ +    return visible;
+ +  }).join("\n");
+ +}

+ +const value = (n: number | null) => n === null ? "미확인" : String(n);
+ +const measured = (m: Measure) => `관측 ${value(m.observed)} / 총량 ${value(m.total)} (${m.coverage})`;
+ +export function formatTeamMetrics(t: TeamMetrics): string {
+ +  const q = t.requests;
+ +  return [
+ +    "## 팀 측정", "",
+ +    `구간: ${t.period.start ?? "시작 미확인"} → ${t.period.end} · ${t.period.unit} · ${t.period.closed ? "닫힘" : "미완료"}`, "",
+ +    `요청 ID별: 도달 관측 ${q.reached} / 총량 ${value(q.reachedTotal)} (사용자 답 ${q.ownerReply}, 사람 경로 성공 ${q.ownerDelivery}) · 실패만 ${q.failedOnly} · 원장 미확인 ${q.unconfirmed} / 비교 미해결 ${q.comparisonUnconfirmed} · 옛 식별자 없음 ${q.legacyUnlinkedEvents}`, "",
+ +    ...(q.attributionUnconfirmed ? [`원장과 로그 연결 미확인: ${q.attributionUnconfirmed}건 · 전체 도달 총량과 비교 방향 보류`, ""] : []),
+ +    `구간 밖 최초 요청의 구간 내 활동: ${q.crossBoundaryObserved}건${q.crossBoundaryObserved ? "(미확인에 포함, 총량/방향 보류)" : ""}`, "",
+ +    `수신 의도: 사용자 ${q.ownerIntendedRequests} / 코디네이터 ${q.coordinatorIntendedRequests} · 질문 사건 ${q.ownerQuestionEvents}/${q.coordinatorQuestionEvents} · 수신 미상 ${q.recipientUnknownEvents}`, "",
+ +    `전달 실패 시도 관측 ${value(q.failedAttemptsObserved)} · 코디네이터 자체 답 관측 ${value(q.coordinatorOwnAnswerObserved)} · 사용자 개입 총합 미확인(열 중복 가능)`,
+ +    "", "| 구분 | 값 |", "|---|---|",
+ +    ...Object.entries(t.manual).map(([name, m]) => `| 손작업 ${name} | ${measured(m)} |`),
+ +    ...Object.entries(t.normal).map(([name, m]) => `| 정상 배정·승인 ${name} | ${measured(m)} |`),
+ +    `| blocking 사건 / 감사 / should_fix 사건 | ${value(t.quality.blockingLogged)} / ${value(t.quality.blockingAudited)} / ${value(t.quality.shouldFixLogged)} |`,
+ +    `| 감사 재검토 / 리뷰 실패 사건 / 게이트 실패 | ${value(t.quality.rereviewsAudited)} / ${value(t.quality.loggedReviewFailures)} / ${value(t.quality.gateFailures)} |`,
+ +    "", "역할별 신고 관측(성적순 아님):", "",
+ +    ...t.roles.map((r) => `- ${r.name}: 작업 ms ${value(r.workMs)} · 출력 ${r.outputTokens.map((o) => `${o.tool} ${value(o.output)}`).join(", ") || "미확인"} · ${r.source}`),
+ +    "", `cwd 구간 근사(역할 귀속 아님): 작업 ms ${value(t.workerContext.workMs)} · ${t.workerContext.outputs.map((o) => `${o.tool} ${value(o.output)}`).join(", ")}`,
+ +    `소통 종류: ${JSON.stringify(t.handoffs.kinds)} · 열린 위임 ${t.handoffs.open.length}`,
+ +    ...Object.entries(t.handoffs.agents).sort(([a], [b]) => a.localeCompare(b)).map(([name, counts]) => `- 소통 ${name}: ${JSON.stringify(counts)}`),
+ +    ...t.unusedRoleCandidates.map((r) => `- 미사용 검토 후보 ${r.role}: ${r.coverage} · ${r.reason}`),
+ +    ...t.warnings.map((warning) => `- 한계: ${warning}`),
+ +  ].join("\n");
+ +}

+ +import type { HandoffRecord } from "../team/handoff-model.ts";

+ +export interface Measure { observed: number | null; total: number | null; coverage: "uncollected" | "reported-partial" | "reported-complete"; refs: string[] }
+ +export interface MeasurementSnapshot {
+ +  version: 1; measurementKind: "stats-reported-v1"; repository: string; condition: string | null; observed_at: string; period: TeamPeriod; comparison: ComparisonContext;
+ +  counts: Record<string, number | null>; observed_counts: Record<string, number | null>; unknown: { request_events: number; recipient_events: number; manual_fields: number }; limits: string[];
+ +}
+ +export interface TeamMetrics {
+ +  version: 1; period: TeamPeriod;
+ +  requests: { linked: number; crossBoundaryObserved: number; attributionUnconfirmed: number; ownerReply: number; ownerDelivery: number; reached: number; reachedTotal: number | null; failedOnly: number; unconfirmed: number; comparisonUnconfirmed: number; legacyUnlinkedEvents: number; ownerIntendedRequests: number; coordinatorIntendedRequests: number; ownerQuestionEvents: number; coordinatorQuestionEvents: number; recipientUnknownEvents: number; failedAttemptsObserved: number | null; coordinatorOwnAnswerObserved: number | null; coordinatorOwnAnswerTotal: null };
+ +  ownerInterventionTotal: null;
+ +  manual: { roleCorrections: Measure; fileEdits: Measure; commandRuns: Measure; extraInstructions: Measure };
+ +  normal: { roleAssignments: Measure; approvals: Measure; mergeApprovals: Measure; deployApprovals: Measure; scopeApprovals: Measure };
+ +  quality: { blockingLogged: number | null; shouldFixLogged: number | null; blockingAudited: number | null; rereviewsAudited: number | null; loggedReviewFailures: number | null; gateFailures: number | null };
+ +  roles: { name: string; workMs: number | null; outputTokens: { tool: string; output: number | null }[]; source: "reported-role-binding" | "unconfirmed"; refs: string[] }[];
+ +  workerContext: { workMs: number | null; outputs: { tool: string; output: number | null }[]; attribution: "cwd-window-proxy-not-role" };
+ +  unusedRoleCandidates: { role: string; coverage: "unconfirmed"; reason: string }[];
+ +  handoffs: { kinds: Record<string, number>; agents: Record<string, Record<string, number>>; open: string[] };
+ +  snapshot: MeasurementSnapshot; warnings: string[];
+ +}
+ +function periodFor(stats: FullStats, input: TeamInput | null, now: string, dir: string, warnings: string[], events: readonly ProjectEvent[], inputs?: InputTrace): TeamPeriod {
+ +  const unit = input?.comparison?.unit ?? input?.period?.unit ?? "project-start-to-merge";
+ +  const nativeTimes = (type: "start" | "merge") => events.filter((e) => e.type === type && e.by === "cli" && e.ok === true && Date.parse(e.ts) <= Date.parse(now)).map((e) => e.ts).sort((a, b) => Date.parse(a) - Date.parse(b));
+ +  const nativeStart = nativeTimes("start")[0] ?? null, terminal = unit === "project-start-to-merge" ? nativeTimes("merge").at(-1) ?? null : stats.project.finish;
+ +  const start = unit === "project-start-to-merge" ? nativeStart : stats.project.start;
+ +  if (start && terminal && Date.parse(terminal) < Date.parse(start)) throw new HandoffError("팀 성공 종료 사건이 시작보다 앞선다");
+ +  if (!input?.period) return { start, end: terminal ?? now, unit, closed: unit === "project-start-to-merge" && !!terminal && !!start };
+ +  const p = { ...input.period };
+ +  if (p.unit !== unit || Date.parse(p.end) > Date.parse(now) || p.start && start && Date.parse(p.start) < Date.parse(start) || terminal && Date.parse(p.end) > Date.parse(terminal)) throw new HandoffError("팀 측정 선언 구간이 프로젝트/조회 범위를 벗어난다");
+ +  if (unit === "project-start-to-merge") {
+ +    if (start && p.start && Date.parse(p.start) !== Date.parse(start)) throw new HandoffError("프로젝트 start→merge 비교 구간 불일치");
+ +    p.start = start ?? p.start; p.end = terminal ?? now; p.closed = !!terminal && !!start;
+ +    if (!terminal || !start) { p.closed = false; warnings.push("실제 성공 start/merge 사건 없음: 프로젝트 전체 범위 미완료"); }
+ +  } else if (!p.completionRef || !p.start) { p.closed = false; warnings.push("대표 업무 완료 구간/metadata 근거 미확인"); }
+ +  else if (!completionCovered(dir, p.completionRef, p, inputs)) { p.closed = false; warnings.push("완료 영수증과 대표 업무 구간 불일치/결합 미확인"); }
+ +  return p;
+ +}
+ +function measure(input: TeamInput | null, key: ManualKey, period: TeamPeriod, dir: string, inputs?: InputTrace): Measure {
+ +  const proposed = input?.counts?.[key] ?? null, values = input?.observed_counts;
+ +  const observed = values && Object.hasOwn(values, key) ? values[key] ?? null : proposed;
+ +  if (proposed !== null && observed !== null && observed > proposed) throw new HandoffError("관측 하한이 신고 총량보다 크다");
+ +  const proof = input?.coverage?.[key], total = period.closed && scopeConfirmed(dir, proof, period, key, inputs) ? proposed : null;
+ +  return { observed, total, coverage: total !== null ? "reported-complete" : observed !== null ? "reported-partial" : "uncollected", refs: proof?.refs ?? [] };
+ +}
+ +export interface TeamCollection { metrics: TeamMetrics; adopted: boolean; handoffs?: HandoffReport }
+ +export function collectTeamMetrics(repo: Repo, ref: ProjectRef, stats: FullStats, options: TokenOptions = {}, inputs?: InputTrace): TeamCollection | undefined {
+ +  const projectDir = `projects/${ref.dir}`, dir = join(repo.root, projectDir), legacyEvents = readEvents(dir, inputs).filter((e) => e.p === ref.id);
+ +  const projectBytes = readFileSync(join(dir, "PROJECT.md")), project = projectBytes.toString("utf8");
+ +  const hasInput = existsSync(join(dir, measurementFile)), hasLedger = existsSync(join(dir, "handoffs.jsonl"));
+ +  const adopted = hasTeamContract(project, hasInput, legacyEvents);
+ +  if (!adopted && !hasLedger && !legacyEvents.some((e) => e.role || e.to || e.requestId)) return undefined;
+ +  safeTeamPath(repo.root, `${projectDir}/PROJECT.md`);
+ +  inputs?.record(join(dir, "PROJECT.md"), inputHash(projectBytes));
+ +  const logEvents = adopted ? readTeamEvents(dir, ref.id, inputs) : legacyEvents, events = (logEvents ?? []).filter((e) => e.p === ref.id);
+ +  const input = readTeamInput(repo.root, projectDir, inputs), now = options.now ?? new Date().toISOString(), warnings: string[] = [];
+ +  if (logEvents === null) warnings.push("사건 로그 미수집: 도달/로그 품질 총량을 확정하지 않는다");
+ +  const period = periodFor(stats, input, now, dir, warnings, events, inputs), low = period.start ? Date.parse(period.start) : -Infinity, high = Date.parse(period.end);
+ +  const inside = (at: string) => Date.parse(at) >= low && Date.parse(at) <= high;
+ +  const scopedEvents = events.filter((e) => inside(e.ts));
+ +  const rows = hasLedger ? new HandoffStore(repo.root, ref.id, projectDir).read(inputs) : [];
+ +  const requestMirrorEvidence = (event: ProjectEvent, proposals: readonly HandoffRecord[]): boolean => {
+ +    if (!event.ref) return false;
+ +    try {
+ +      const proof = new InputSnapshot(), receipt = evidenceReceipt(dir, event.ref, proof);
+ +      const proposal = proposals.find((r) => receipt.sourceRef === `handoff:${r.id}`);
+ +      if (!proposal || receipt.requestId !== proposal.requestId || receipt.hash !== proposal.bodyHash ||
+ +          receipt.decisionReplyId !== undefined || (event.t !== undefined && event.t !== proposal.task) ||
+ +          Date.parse(event.ts) < Date.parse(proposal.at)) return false;
+ +      const range = receipt.period as { start: string; end: string; unit: string } | undefined;
+ +      if (range && (range.unit !== period.unit || Date.parse(event.ts) < Date.parse(range.start) || Date.parse(event.ts) > Date.parse(range.end))) return false;
+ +      proof.include(inputs);
+ +      return true;
+ +    } catch (error) { if (error instanceof InputSnapshotError) throw error; return false; }
+ +  };
+ +  const handoff = collectHandoffs(repo.root, ref.id, projectDir, { start: period.start, end: period.end, eventSnapshot: events, rowSnapshot: rows, inputs, ...(adopted ? { requestMirrorEvidence } : {}), ownerAnswerEvidence: (e, requestAt) => {
+ +    try {
+ +      if (!e.requestId || !e.ref) return false;
+ +      const proof = new InputSnapshot(), receipt = evidenceReceipt(dir, e.ref, proof);
+ +      if (!receiptPurpose(receipt, "owner-answer", e.requestId)) return false;
+ +      const receiptPeriod = receipt.period as { start: string; end: string; unit: string } | undefined;
+ +      if (receiptPeriod && (Date.parse(e.ts) < Date.parse(receiptPeriod.start) || Date.parse(e.ts) > Date.parse(receiptPeriod.end) || receiptPeriod.unit !== period.unit)) return false;
+ +      if (receipt.decisionReplyId && !rows.some((r) => r.id === receipt.decisionReplyId && r.requestId === e.requestId && ownerReplyAfterProposal(r, rows, requestAt) && inside(r.at) && Date.parse(r.at) <= Date.parse(e.ts))) return false;
+ +      proof.include(inputs);
+ +      return true;
+ +    } catch (error) { if (error instanceof InputSnapshotError) throw error; return false; }
+ +  } });
+ +  const ids = new Set(handoff?.requests.items.map((r) => r.requestId) ?? []), periodRows = rows.filter((r) => inside(r.at)), admitted = periodRows.filter((r) => !r.requestId || ids.has(r.requestId));
+ +  if (handoff?.requests.items.some((r) => r.scopeConflict)) warnings.push("같은 요청 ID의 명시 태스크 범위 충돌: 총량을 미확인으로 둔다");
+ +  const ambiguous = handoff?.requests.items.filter((r) => r.attributionUnconfirmed) ?? [];
+ +  if (ambiguous.length) warnings.push("원장과 질문 로그의 연결 미확인: 원장 도달 관측은 보존하고 전체 총량과 비교 방향을 보류한다");
+ +  const crossBoundaryObserved = handoff?.crossBoundaryRequests?.length ?? 0;
+ +  if (crossBoundaryObserved) warnings.push("최초 요청이 구간 밖인 활동이 있어 도달 총량/부담 방향을 보류한다. 역할·시도·소통 사건 관측은 실제 구간을 따른다");
+ +  const questions = scopedEvents.filter((e) => ["question", "escalation"].includes(e.type));
+ +  const own = new Set<string>(), trustedOwn = new Set<string>();
+ +  for (const answer of input?.coordinator_answers ?? []) {
+ +    const request = handoff?.requests.items.find((r) => r.requestId === answer.requestId);
+ +    if (request && !["owner_reply", "owner_delivery"].includes(request.reach) && request.ownerSignal === false && !request.scopeConflict && !request.attributionUnconfirmed && request.coordinatorAnswered && requestCovered(dir, answer.requestId, answer.coverage, inputs)) {
+ +      own.add(answer.requestId);
+ +      if (scopedRequestCovered(dir, answer.requestId, answer.coverage, period, inputs)) trustedOwn.add(answer.requestId);
+ +    }
+ +  }
+ +  const intended = (to: "owner" | "coordinator") => new Set([
+ +    ...questions.filter((e) => e.to === to && e.requestId && ids.has(e.requestId)).map((e) => e.requestId!),
+ +    ...admitted.filter((r) => r.kind === "decision_request" && r.to === to && r.requestId).map((r) => r.requestId!),
+ +  ]).size;
+ +  const requests: TeamMetrics["requests"] = {
+ +    linked: (handoff?.requests.total ?? 0) + crossBoundaryObserved, crossBoundaryObserved, attributionUnconfirmed: ambiguous.length, ownerReply: handoff?.requests.owner_reply ?? 0, ownerDelivery: handoff?.requests.owner_delivery ?? 0,
+ +    reachedTotal: null,
+ +    reached: (handoff?.requests.owner_reply ?? 0) + (handoff?.requests.owner_delivery ?? 0), failedOnly: handoff?.requests.failed_only ?? 0, unconfirmed: (handoff?.requests.unconfirmed ?? 0) + crossBoundaryObserved, comparisonUnconfirmed: 0,
+ +    legacyUnlinkedEvents: handoff?.requests.unlinkedLogEvents ?? scopedEvents.filter((e) => !e.requestId && (["question", "escalation"].includes(e.type) || e.type === "answer" && e.by === "user")).length,
+ +    ownerIntendedRequests: intended("owner"),
+ +    coordinatorIntendedRequests: intended("coordinator"),
+ +    ownerQuestionEvents: questions.filter((e) => e.to === "owner").length, coordinatorQuestionEvents: questions.filter((e) => e.to === "coordinator").length, recipientUnknownEvents: questions.filter((e) => !e.to).length,
+ +    failedAttemptsObserved: hasLedger ? (handoff?.failedRequestAttempts ?? 0) : null,
+ +    coordinatorOwnAnswerObserved: input?.coordinator_answers ? own.size : null, coordinatorOwnAnswerTotal: null,
+ +  };
+ +  requests.comparisonUnconfirmed = requests.unconfirmed + ambiguous.filter((r) => r.reach !== "unconfirmed").length - (period.closed && scopeConfirmed(dir, input?.request_coverage, period, "request-coverage", inputs) ? [...trustedOwn].filter((id) => handoff?.requests.items.some((r) => r.requestId === id && r.reach === "unconfirmed")).length : 0);
+ +  const requestComplete = logEvents !== null && period.closed && scopeConfirmed(dir, input?.request_coverage, period, "request-coverage", inputs);
+ +  if (requestComplete && requests.comparisonUnconfirmed === 0 && requests.legacyUnlinkedEvents === 0) requests.reachedTotal = requests.reached;
+ +  requests.comparisonUnconfirmed += requests.failedOnly;
+ +  const measures = Object.fromEntries(MANUAL_KEYS.map((key) => [key, measure(input, key, period, dir, inputs)])) as Record<ManualKey, Measure>;
+ +  const audit = input?.quality_audit, auditConfirmed = period.closed && scopeConfirmed(dir, audit?.coverage, period, "quality-audit", inputs);
+ +  const reviewRows = scopedEvents.filter((e) => e.type === "review"), gateRows = scopedEvents.filter((e) => e.type === "gate");
+ +  const logComplete = logEvents !== null && period.closed && scopeConfirmed(dir, input?.log_coverage, period, "log-coverage", inputs);
+ +  if (!logComplete) warnings.push("현재 구간의 로그 수집 완전성 미확인");
+ +  const reviewsComplete = logComplete && reviewRows.every(completeReviewEvent);
+ +  if (!reviewsComplete && reviewRows.length) warnings.push("리뷰 수치/판정 필드 미수집: 로그 품질을 미확인으로 둔다");
+ +  const reviewSum = (key: "blocking" | "should_fix"): number | null => {
+ +    if (!reviewsComplete) return null;
+ +    let total = 0; for (const event of reviewRows) { total += event.data![key]!; if (!Number.isSafeInteger(total)) return null; } return total;
+ +  };
+ +  const quality = { blockingLogged: reviewSum("blocking"), shouldFixLogged: reviewSum("should_fix"), blockingAudited: auditConfirmed ? audit!.blocking_sum : null, rereviewsAudited: auditConfirmed ? audit!.rereviews_after_fail : null, loggedReviewFailures: !reviewsComplete ? null : reviewRows.filter((e) => e.data?.fail === 1).length, gateFailures: !logComplete || gateRows.some((e) => { const cmd = Object.keys(e.data ?? {}); return cmd.length !== 1 || !["check", "done", "finish"].includes(cmd[0]!) || e.data?.[cmd[0]!] !== 1 || (e.ok === true ? e.msg !== cmd[0] : e.ok !== false || !new RegExp(`^${cmd[0]}: (?:security|rebase|checks|contract|state|command)$`).test(e.msg)); }) ? null : gateRows.filter((e) => e.ok === false).length };
+ +  const members = loadTeam(repo.root, inputs), names = new Set([...members.map((m) => m.name), ...events.flatMap((e) => e.role ? [e.role.name] : [])]);
+ +  for (const observation of input?.role_observations ?? []) if (!names.has(observation.role)) throw new HandoffError("역할 결합 관측의 이름이 원본/시작 기록에 없다");
+ +  const roles: TeamMetrics["roles"] = [...names].sort().map((name) => {

+ +    if (r && !hasProof) warnings.push(`역할 ${name}: 현재 구간에 결합된 관측 근거 미확인`);
+ +    return { name, workMs: hasProof ? r!.workMs : null, outputTokens: hasProof ? r!.outputTokens : [], source: hasProof ? "reported-role-binding" : "unconfirmed", refs: r?.coverage.refs ?? [] };
+ +  });
+ +  const declared = new Set([...scopedEvents.flatMap((e) => e.role ? [e.role.name] : []), ...periodRows.flatMap((r) => [r.from, r.to].filter((a) => a.startsWith("agent:")).map((a) => a.slice(6)))]);

+ +  if (!period.start || !period.closed) warnings.push("닫힌 동일 비교 범위 미확인");
+ +  if (handoff?.auditCoverage === "metadata-only") warnings.push("로컬 본문 없음: metadata 신고 근거만 남아 원문 재감사 불가");
+ +  warnings.push("신고 actor/ref와 complete는 사람 인증이 아니며 실제 완전성은 현장 감사 대상", "질문·승인·손작업 열은 겹칠 수 있어 사용자 개입 총합을 내지 않는다", "cwd/구간 토큰과 작업 시간은 역할별 실제 사용량이 아니다");
+ +  const counts: Record<string, number | null> = { owner_questions: requests.reachedTotal, coordinator_questions: requests.coordinatorIntendedRequests, recipient_unknown: requests.recipientUnknownEvents, failed_owner_delivery_attempts: requests.failedAttemptsObserved, coordinator_answers: requests.coordinatorOwnAnswerObserved, blocking_sum_stats_events: quality.blockingLogged, should_fix_sum_stats_events: quality.shouldFixLogged, blocking_sum_audited: quality.blockingAudited, rereviews_after_fail: quality.rereviewsAudited, review_failures: quality.loggedReviewFailures, gate_failures: quality.gateFailures };
+ +  const observed_counts: Record<string, number | null> = { owner_questions: requests.reached };
+ +  counts.failed_only_owner_requests = requestComplete ? requests.failedOnly : null;
+ +  observed_counts.failed_only_owner_requests = requests.failedOnly;
+ +  for (const key of MANUAL_KEYS) { counts[key] = measures[key].total; observed_counts[key] = measures[key].observed; }

+ +  const snapshot: MeasurementSnapshot = { version: 1, measurementKind: "stats-reported-v1", repository: repo.mainRoot, condition: input?.comparison?.conditionCode ?? null, observed_at: now, period, comparison: input?.comparison ?? {}, counts, observed_counts, unknown: { request_events: requests.comparisonUnconfirmed + requests.legacyUnlinkedEvents, recipient_events: requests.recipientUnknownEvents, manual_fields: MANUAL_KEYS.filter((key) => measures[key].total === null).length }, limits: warnings };

[] }, snapshot, warnings };
+ +  return { metrics, adopted, ...(handoff ? { handoffs: handoff } : {}) };
+ +}

+ +import { teamMarkdown } from "./team-markdown.ts";
+ +import { inputHash, InputSnapshotError, type InputTrace } from "../core/input-snapshot.ts";
+ +import { existsSync, readFileSync } from "node:fs";
+ +import { join } from "node:path";
+ +import { writeFileAtomic } from "../core/fsutil.ts";

+ +import { HandoffStore } from "./handoff-store.ts";
+ +import { readEvents } from "./event-log.ts";
+ +import { fields, HandoffError, identifier } from "./handoff-files.ts";

+ +export function adoptTeamMetrics(root: string, projectDir: string): void {
+ +  const path = safeTeamPath(root, `${projectDir}/PROJECT.md`), text = readFileSync(path, "utf8");
+ +  if (teamMarker(text)) return;
+ +  const newline = text.includes("\r\n") ? "\r\n" : "\n";
+ +  const next = text.replace(/^(# [^\r\n]+)(\r?\n)/, `$1$2${newline}- 팀 측정: v1${newline}`);
+ +  if (next === text) throw new HandoffError("팀 측정 marker를 넣을 프로젝트 표제가 없다");
+ +  writeFileAtomic(path, next);
+ +}
+ +export function teamRetrospectiveRequired(root: string, projectDir: string, inputs?: InputTrace): boolean {
+ +  const dir = join(root, projectDir), path = join(dir, "PROJECT.md"), bytes = readFileSync(path);
+ +  inputs?.record(path, inputHash(bytes));
+ +  const required = hasTeamContract(bytes.toString("utf8"), existsSync(join(dir, measurementFile)), readEvents(dir, inputs).filter((event) => event.p === projectDir.split("/").at(-1)!.slice(0, 4)));
+ +  if (required) safeTeamPath(root, `${projectDir}/PROJECT.md`);
+ +  return required;
+ +}
+ +/** Validates metadata only. It neither loads private bodies nor applies proposed role changes. */
+ +export interface KnownFinding { kind: string; code: string; count: number }
+ +export function findingCode(f: KnownFinding): string { return `${f.kind}-${f.code.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-").replace(/^-|-$/g, "")}`; }
+ +function tableCells(line: string): string[] | null {
+ +  const source = line.replace(/[ \t]+$/, "");
+ +  if (!source.startsWith("|")) return null;
+ +  const cells: string[] = []; let start = 1, backslashes = 0;
+ +  for (let i = 1; i < source.length; i++) {
+ +    const char = source[i];
+ +    if (char === "\\") { backslashes++; continue; }
+ +    if (char === "|" && backslashes % 2 === 0) { cells.push(source.slice(start, i).replace(/^[ \t]+|[ \t]+$/g, "")); start = i + 1; }
+ +    backslashes = 0;
+ +  }
+ +  return start === source.length ? cells : null;
+ +}
+ +function cellInk(value: string): boolean {
+ +  // Default-ignorables and BRAILLE PATTERN BLANK cannot establish visible cell content.
+ +  let visible = value.replace(/[\s\p{M}\p{Default_Ignorable_Code_Point}\u2800]/gu, "");
+ +  if (/[\p{M}\p{Default_Ignorable_Code_Point}\u2800]/u.test(value)) visible = visible.replace(/[*_~\[\]]/g, "");
+ +  return !!visible;
+ +}
+ +function escapedPunctuation(value: string, at: number): boolean {
+ +  const n = value.charCodeAt(at + 1);
+ +  return value[at] === "\\" && (n >= 33 && n <= 47 || n >= 58 && n <= 64 || n >= 91 && n <= 96 || n >= 123 && n <= 126);
+ +}
+ +function bracketEnd(value: string, at: number): number {
+ +  let depth = 1;
+ +  for (let i = at + 1; i < value.length; i++) {
+ +    if (escapedPunctuation(value, i)) { i++; continue; }
+ +    if (value[i] === "[") depth++;
+ +    else if (value[i] === "]" && --depth === 0) return i;
+ +  }
+ +  return -1;
+ +}
+ +function destinationEnd(value: string, at: number): number {
+ +  const spaces = (pos: number) => { while (value[pos] === " " || value[pos] === "\t") pos++; return pos; };
+ +  const title = (pos: number): number => {
+ +    const opener = value[pos], closer = opener === "(" ? ")" : opener;
+ +    if (opener !== '"' && opener !== "'" && opener !== "(") return -1;
+ +    for (let i = pos + 1; i < value.length; i++) {
+ +      if (escapedPunctuation(value, i)) { i++; continue; }
+ +      if (value[i] === closer) return i + 1;
+ +      if (value[i] === "\r" || value[i] === "\n" || opener === "(" && value[i] === "(") return -1;
+ +    }
+ +    return -1;
+ +  };
+ +  const finishTitle = (pos: number) => { const end = title(pos); return end >= 0 && value[spaces(end)] === ")" ? spaces(end) : -1; };
+ +  const start = spaces(at + 1);
+ +  let pos = start, depth = 0;
+ +  if (value[pos] === "<") {
+ +    for (pos++; pos < value.length && value[pos] !== ">"; pos++) {
+ +      if (escapedPunctuation(value, pos)) { pos++; continue; }
+ +      if (value[pos] === "<" || value[pos] === "\r" || value[pos] === "\n") return -1;
+ +    }
+ +    if (value[pos++] !== ">") return -1;
+ +  } else {
+ +    for (; pos < value.length; pos++) {
+ +      if (escapedPunctuation(value, pos)) { pos++; continue; }
+ +      const char = value[pos]!;
+ +      if (char === " " || char === "\t" || char === ")" && depth === 0) break;
+ +      if (char.charCodeAt(0) < 32 || char.charCodeAt(0) === 127) return -1;
+ +      if (char === "(" && ++depth > 16) return -1;
+ +      if (char === ")") depth--;
+ +    }
+ +  }
+ +  if (depth === 0) {
+ +    const next = spaces(pos);
+ +    if (value[next] === ")") return next;
+ +    if (next > pos) { const end = finishTitle(next); if (end >= 0) return end; }
+ +  }
+ +  // Empty destination followed by a title is also valid CommonMark.
+ +  return finishTitle(start);
+ +}
+ +/** Projects the supported cell text without borrowing destination/title text as label ink. */
+ +function cellText(value: string, depth = 0): { text: string; valid: boolean } {
+ +  if (depth > 16) return { text: "", valid: false };
+ +  let text = "";
+ +  for (let i = 0; i < value.length;) {
+ +    if (escapedPunctuation(value, i)) { text += "x"; i += 2; continue; }
+ +    if (value[i] !== "[") { text += value[i++]!; continue; }
+ +    const end = bracketEnd(value, i);
+ +    if (end < 0) return { text, valid: false };
+ +    const label = cellText(value.slice(i + 1, end), depth + 1);
+ +    if (!label.valid) return { text, valid: false };
+ +    if (value[i - 1] === "!" && text.endsWith("!")) return { text, valid: false };
+ +    let next = end + 1, target = -1;
+ +    if (value[next] === "(") target = destinationEnd(value, next);
+ +    else { while (value[next] === " " || value[next] === "\t") next++; if (value[next] === "[") target = bracketEnd(value, next); }
+ +    if (value[end + 1] === "(" || value[next] === "[") {
+ +      if (target < 0 || !cellInk(label.text)) return { text, valid: false };
+ +      text += label.text; i = target + 1;
+ +    } else { text += `[${label.text}]`; i = end + 1; }
+ +  }
+ +  return { text, valid: true };
+ +}
+ +function usableText(value: string): boolean {
+ +  const projected = cellText(value);
+ +  return projected.valid && cellInk(projected.text) && !/[\p{Cc}\p{Cf}]/u.test(value) && !projected.text.includes("`") &&
+ +    !/<!--|<\?|<![A-Za-z]|<!\[CDATA\[|<\/?[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9-]*(?=[ \t/>])(?:"[^"]*"|'[^']*'|[^'"<>])*?>|&(?:#[xX]?[a-fA-F0-9]+|[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9]+);/.test(projected.text);
+ +}
+ +function visibleTeamRows(close: string): string[] {
+ +  const lines: string[] = [], rows: string[] = []; let active = false;
+ +  for (const line of teamMarkdown(close, true).split("\n")) {
+ +    if (active && /^#{1,2}(?:[ \t]|$)/.test(line)) break;
+ +    if (!active) { const heading = /^##[ \t]+회고(?:[ \t]+#+)?[ \t]*$/.exec(line); active = heading?.[0] === line; }
+ +    else lines.push(line);
+ +  }
+ +  if (lines.includes("<!-- unsupported underline -->")) throw new HandoffError("회고 표 구간은 ATX 제목만 사용한다: 밑줄·구분선·빈 목록처럼 기호만 있는 지원 밖 행을 ATX 제목 또는 빈 줄로 정리한다");
+ +  if (lines.includes("<!-- unsupported indented heading -->")) throw new HandoffError("회고 기계 구간의 제목은 열0에 쓴다: 목록 안/들여쓴 제목을 일반 텍스트로 정리한다");
+ +  for (let i = 0; i + 2 < lines.length; i++) {
+ +    if (!lines[i]!.startsWith("|")) continue;
+ +    let end = i + 1;
+ +    while (end < lines.length && lines[end]!.startsWith("|")) end++;
+ +    const header = tableCells(lines[i]!), separator = tableCells(lines[i + 1]!);
+ +    const accepted = header?.length === 3 && header.every(usableText) && separator?.length === 3 && separator.every((cell) => /^:?-{3,}:?$/.test(cell));
+ +    if (accepted) for (let j = i + 2; j < end; j++) { const cells = tableCells(lines[j]!); if (cells?.length === 3 && cells[0] === "팀") rows.push(lines[j]!); }
+ +    i = end - 1;
+ +  }
+ +  return rows;
+ +}
+ +export function validateTeamRetrospective(root: string, projectDir: string, close: string, findings: KnownFinding[] = [], candidateRoles?: string[], period?: TeamPeriod, inputs?: InputTrace): void {
+ +  const dir = join(root, projectDir);
+ +  const rows = visibleTeamRows(close);
+ +  const row = rows.length === 1 ? tableCells(rows[0]!) : null;
+ +  const summary = row?.[2]?.trim();
+ +  const label = row?.[1]?.match(/^\[([^\]]+)\]\(TEAM-RETROSPECTIVE\.json\)$/)?.[1];
+ +  if (row?.length !== 3 || row[0] !== "팀" || !label || !usableText(label) || !summary || !usableText(summary)) throw new HandoffError("PROJECT-CLOSE 회고에 팀 행·고정 JSON 링크·보이는 일반 텍스트가 필요하다: 빈 링크/제어 문자/HTML/주석/엔터티/inline 코드는 일반 텍스트로 정리한다");
+ +  const v = metadataJson(safeTeamPath(root, `${projectDir}/${retrospectiveFile}`), 32768, inputs);
+ +  fields(v, ["version", "metricRef", "unusedRoles", "recurringFindings", "definitionChanges", "noChangesReasonRef"], ["version", "metricRef", "unusedRoles", "recurringFindings", "definitionChanges"]);
+ +  if (v.version !== 1 || v.metricRef !== "METRICS.md") throw new HandoffError("팀 회고 version/metricRef 오류");
+ +  // METRICS.md is a generated destination; first finish must not require it to exist yet.
+ +  safeTeamPath(root, `${projectDir}/METRICS.md`);
+ +  for (const key of ["unusedRoles", "recurringFindings", "definitionChanges"]) if (!Array.isArray(v[key]) || v[key].length > (key === "definitionChanges" ? 3 : 32)) throw new HandoffError("팀 회고 목록/정의 변경 개수 오류");
+ +  const roles = new Set<string>();
+ +  for (const r of v.unusedRoles as unknown[]) {
+ +    fields(r, ["role", "coverage", "refs"]);
+ +    if (!identifier(r.role) || roles.has(r.role) || !["observed-none", "unconfirmed"].includes(r.coverage as string)) throw new HandoffError("미사용 후보 역할/관측 범위 오류");
+ +    roles.add(r.role); const refs = proofRefs(dir, r.refs, inputs);
+ +    if (r.coverage === "observed-none" && (!period || !refs.some((ref) => observationCovered(dir, ref, period, r.role as string, "none", inputs)))) throw new HandoffError("관측 없음 후보는 현재 구간의 범위 근거가 필요하다");
+ +  }
+ +  const codes = new Set<string>();
+ +  for (const r of v.recurringFindings as unknown[]) {
+ +    fields(r, ["code", "count", "coverage", "refs"]);
+ +    if (!identifier(r.code) || codes.has(r.code) || !natural(r.count) || r.count < 2 || r.coverage !== "reported" || !proofRefs(dir, r.refs, inputs).length) throw new HandoffError("반복 지적은 신고 count≥2와 근거가 필요하다");
+ +    codes.add(r.code);
+ +  }
+ +  const events = readEvents(dir, inputs), declared = new Set(events.flatMap((e) => e.role ? [e.role.name] : []));
+ +  const ledger = safeTeamPath(root, `${projectDir}/handoffs.jsonl`);
+ +  if (existsSync(ledger)) for (const r of new HandoffStore(root, projectDir.split("/").at(-1)!.slice(0, 4), projectDir).read(inputs)) for (const a of [r.from, r.to]) if (a.startsWith("agent:")) declared.add(a.slice(6));
+ +  const members = loadTeam(root, inputs);
+ +  const expectedRoles = candidateRoles ?? members.filter((m) => !declared.has(m.name)).map((m) => m.name);
+ +  if (candidateRoles) for (const entry of v.unusedRoles as Record<string, unknown>[]) if (entry.coverage === "observed-none" && !candidateRoles.includes(entry.role as string)) throw new HandoffError("현재 관측/참여 역할은 관측 없음 후보로 신고할 수 없다");
+ +  for (const name of expectedRoles) if (!roles.has(name)) throw new HandoffError("회고에 알려진 미사용 검토 후보가 빠졌다");
+ +  for (const f of findings.filter((f) => ["gate", "review"].includes(f.kind) && f.count >= 2)) {
+ +    const entry = (v.recurringFindings as Record<string, unknown>[]).find((r) => r.code === findingCode(f));
+ +    if (!entry || entry.count !== f.count) throw new HandoffError("회고의 반복 지적이 현재 지표 후보와 불일치한다");
+ +  }
+ +  const changes = v.definitionChanges as unknown[];
+ +  const knownRoles = new Set([...members.map((m) => m.name), ...declared]);
+ +  if (changes.length === 0) evidenceReceipt(dir, v.noChangesReasonRef, inputs);
+ +  else {
+ +    if (v.noChangesReasonRef !== undefined) throw new HandoffError("정의 변경과 무변경 이유를 함께 둘 수 없다");
+ +    for (const c of changes) { fields(c, ["role", "changeRef", "reasonRef"]); if (!identifier(c.role) || !knownRoles.has(c.role)) throw new HandoffError("정의 변경 역할은 팀 원본/참여 기록에 있어야 한다"); evidenceReceipt(dir, c.changeRef, inputs); evidenceReceipt(dir, c.reasonRef, inputs); }
+ +  }
+ +}
+ +export function teamRetrospectiveIssues(root: string, projectDir: string, findings: KnownFinding[] = [], candidateRoles?: string[], period?: TeamPeriod, inputs?: InputTrace, requireAdoption = false): string[] {
+ +  try {
+ +    if (!teamRetrospectiveRequired(root, projectDir, inputs)) return requireAdoption ? ["팀 채택 근거가 검사 중 사라졌다: 팀 측정 입력을 복원하고 다시 마감한다"] : [];
+ +    const bytes = readFileSync(safeTeamPath(root, `${projectDir}/PROJECT-CLOSE.md`)), close = bytes.toString("utf8");
+ +    inputs?.record(join(root, projectDir, "PROJECT-CLOSE.md"), inputHash(bytes));
+ +    validateTeamRetrospective(root, projectDir, close, findings, candidateRoles, period, inputs); return [];
+ +  } catch (e) { return [`팀 회고: ${e instanceof HandoffError || e instanceof InputSnapshotError ? e.message : "고정 파일 누락/안전 경계 오류"}`]; }
+ +}

+ +  const dir = join(root, "projects", readdirSync(join(root, "projects")).find((p) => p.startsWith("P001_"))!);
+ +  writeFileSync(join(dir, "log.jsonl"), ""); // Explicit empty collection; missing and corrupt sources have separate negative cases.
+ +  return { root, home, dir, run, git };

+ +test("팀 stats는 수신 의도만 있는 요청을 도달로 세지 않고 없는 손작업 총량을0으로 채우지 않는다", () => {

+ +import { compareTeam, formatTeamComparison } from "../src/project/team-comparison.ts";
+ +import { teamRetrospectiveIssues, adoptTeamMetrics } from "../src/project/team-retrospective.ts";
+ +import { digest } from "../src/project/handoff-files.ts";
+ +import { recordEvent, reviewMessageData } from "../src/project/event-log.ts";
+ +import { EVIDENCE_PURPOSES, MANUAL_KEYS } from "../src/project/team-input.ts";
+ +import { symlinkSync, linkSync, unlinkSync } from "node:fs";
+ +
+ +function receipt(dir: string, name = "proof.json", period?: { start: string | null; end: string; unit: string }, requestId?: string) {
+ +  writeFileSync(join(dir, name), JSON.stringify({ version: 1, kind: "team-evidence", purposes: requestId ? ["owner-answer", "coordinator-answer"] : EVIDENCE_PURPOSES.filter((p) => !["owner-answer", "coordinator-answer"].includes(p)), sourceRef: "fixture:synthetic-metadata", hash: digest(`synthetic-local-not-copied:${JSON.stringify(period ?? null)}`), ...(requestId ? { requestId } : {}), ...(period ? { period: { start: period.start, end: period.end, unit: period.unit } } : {}) })); return name;
+ +}
+ +function roleReceipt(dir: string, name: string, period: { start: string | null; end: string; unit: string }, role: string, kind: "activity" | "none") {
+ +  receipt(dir, name, period);
+ +  const path = join(dir, name), proof = JSON.parse(readFileSync(path, "utf8"));
+ +  proof.sourceRef = `role-observation:${role}:${kind}:fixture:observation`;
+ +  writeFileSync(path, JSON.stringify(proof));
+ +}
+ +function snapshot(repository = "fixture-repo", after = false) {
+ +  const counts: Record<string, number> = Object.fromEntries(MANUAL_KEYS.map((key) => [key, 5]));
+ +  Object.assign(counts, { owner_questions: after ? 1 : 5, failed_only_owner_requests: 0, blocking_sum_stats_events: 3, rereviews_after_fail: 2, review_failures: 0, should_fix_sum_stats_events: 0, gate_failures: 1 });
+ +  return { version: 1, repository, comparison: { study: "team-adoption", conditionCode: after ? "team" : "no-team", definition: "reach-v1", unit: "representative-task", workType: "feature", sizeBand: "small", host: "codex", sampleCount: 2, recordingBoundary: "closed-task", qualitySource: "event-log", qualityScopeHash: digest("same-quality-scope") }, period: { start: "2026-01-01T00:00:00Z", end: "2026-01-02T00:00:00Z", unit: "representative-task", closed: true, completionRef: "completion.json" }, counts, observed_counts: {}, unknown: { request_events: 0 } };
+ +}
+ +function compareFixture() {
+ +  const dir = tempDir("pace-team-comparison-"), before = join(dir, "before.json"), after = join(dir, "after.json");
+ +  const b = snapshot(), a = snapshot("fixture-repo", true); receipt(dir, "completion.json", b.period);
+ +  const group = (s: ReturnType<typeof snapshot>, n: number) => ({ version: 1, samples: Array.from({ length: n }, (_, i) => {
+ +    const period = { ...s.period, start: `2026-01-0${i * 2 + 1 + (s.comparison.conditionCode === "team" ? 4 : 0)}T00:00:00Z`, end: `2026-01-0${i * 2 + 2 + (s.comparison.conditionCode === "team" ? 4 : 0)}T00:00:00Z`, completionRef: `completion-${s.comparison.conditionCode}-${i}.json` };
+ +    receipt(dir, period.completionRef, period); return { ...s, period };
+ +  }) });
+ +  const save = (n = 2) => { writeFileSync(before, JSON.stringify(group(b, n))); writeFileSync(after, JSON.stringify(group(a, n))); };
+ +  save(); return { dir, before, after, b, a, save, compare: () => compareTeam(before, after, "team-adoption") };
+ +}
+ +test("팀 비교는 부담 개선·악화·품질 변화 미확인을 분리하며 품질 감소를 개선으로 포장하지 않는다", () => {
+ +  const f = compareFixture(); assert.equal(f.compare().conclusion, "observed-burden-improvement");
+ +  assert.match(formatTeamComparison(f.compare()), /\| 지표 \|[^\n]*\n\|---\|/);
+ +  f.a.counts.owner_questions = 8; f.save(); assert.equal(f.compare().conclusion, "observed-burden-worsening");
+ +  f.a.counts.owner_questions = 1; f.a.counts.blocking_sum_stats_events = 1; f.save();
+ +  const r = f.compare(); assert.equal(r.conclusion, "unknown"); assert.equal(r.rows.find((r) => r.metric === "blocking_sum")?.delta, -4); assert.ok(r.reasons.some((s) => s.includes("품질 지표 변화")));
+ +});
+ +test("표본·정의·범위·저장소 누락/불일치와 혼재 증감을 판정 전에 검출한다", () => {
+ +  const f = compareFixture(); f.a.comparison.sampleCount = 99; f.save(1); assert.equal(f.compare().conclusion, "unknown");
+ +  f.a.comparison.sampleCount = 2; f.a.counts.owner_file_edits = 8; f.save(); assert.match(f.compare().reasons.join(" "), /혼재/);
+ +  f.a.repository = "other-repo"; f.save(); assert.ok(f.compare().rows.every((r) => r.delta === null));
+ +  f.a.repository = "fixture-repo"; f.a.comparison.definition = "different"; f.save(); assert.match(f.compare().reasons.join(" "), /definition 불일치/);
+ +  f.a.comparison.conditionCode = "after-change"; f.save(); assert.throws(() => f.compare(), /목적\/전후 조건/);
+ +});
+ +test("legacy 기준선 원본을 바꾸지 않고 지문 고정 주석을 요구하며 수신 의도를 도달로 승격하지 않는다", () => {
+ +  const f = compareFixture(); const raw = { version: 1, repository: "fixture-repo", condition: "한글 메시지 역할 조건", counts: { captain_questions: 10, captain_questions_range: [10, 12], captain_manual_role_corrections: null, blocking_sum_audited: 48, blocking_sum_stats_events: 31, rereviews_after_fail_audited: 18 }, unknown: { recipient_events: 2 } };
+ +  writeFileSync(f.before, JSON.stringify(raw)); const original = readFileSync(f.before);
+ +  const r = f.compare(); assert.equal(r.conclusion, "unknown"); assert.equal(r.sources.before.counts.owner_questions, null); assert.deepEqual(r.sources.before.range, [10, 12]); assert.equal(r.sources.before.counts.blocking_sum_audited, 48); assert.equal(r.sources.before.counts.blocking_sum_stats_events, 31);
+ +  const ann = join(f.dir, "annotation.json"); writeFileSync(ann, JSON.stringify({ version: 1, sourceSha256: digest("wrong"), sourceRef: "fixture:annotation", comparison: f.b.comparison }));
+ +  assert.throws(() => compareTeam(f.before, f.after, "team-adoption", ann), /지문/);
+ +  writeFileSync(ann, JSON.stringify({ version: 1, sourceSha256: digest(original), sourceRef: "fixture:annotation", comparison: f.b.comparison }));
+ +  assert.equal(compareTeam(f.before, f.after, "team-adoption", ann).sources.before.counts.owner_questions, null); assert.deepEqual(readFileSync(f.before), original);
+ +});
+ +test("회고는 최초 METRICS 생성 전에 metadata 근거로 검증하며 원문 경로·빈 이유·4변경을 거부한다", () => {
+ +  const f = fixture(), rel = `projects/${f.dir.split("/").at(-1)}`;
+ +  createTeamMember(f.root, "maker", "maker");
+ +  assert.deepEqual(teamRetrospectiveIssues(f.root, rel), []); adoptTeamMetrics(f.root, rel);
+ +  assert.match(teamRetrospectiveIssues(f.root, rel).join(" "), /회고/);
+ +  const proof = receipt(f.dir), close = "## 회고\n\n| 구분 | 입력 | 요약 |\n|---|---|---|\n| 팀 | [팀 회고](TEAM-RETROSPECTIVE.json) | 합성 범위 검토 |\n";
+ +  writeFileSync(join(f.dir, "PROJECT-CLOSE.md"), close);
+ +  const data = { version: 1, metricRef: "METRICS.md", unusedRoles: [{ role: "maker", coverage: "unconfirmed", refs: [] }], recurringFindings: [{ code: "scope", count: 2, coverage: "reported", refs: [proof] }], definitionChanges: [] as unknown[], noChangesReasonRef: proof as string | undefined };
+ +  const save = () => writeFileSync(join(f.dir, "TEAM-RETROSPECTIVE.json"), JSON.stringify(data)); save(); assert.deepEqual(teamRetrospectiveIssues(f.root, rel), []);
+ +  data.noChangesReasonRef = ""; save(); assert.ok(teamRetrospectiveIssues(f.root, rel).length);
+ +  data.noChangesReasonRef = undefined; data.definitionChanges = Array(4).fill({ role: "maker", changeRef: proof, reasonRef: proof }); save(); assert.ok(teamRetrospectiveIssues(f.root, rel).length);
+ +  data.definitionChanges = [{ role: "maker", changeRef: proof, reasonRef: proof }]; save(); assert.deepEqual(teamRetrospectiveIssues(f.root, rel), []);
+ +  data.definitionChanges = [{ role: "not-a-team-role", changeRef: proof, reasonRef: proof }]; save(); assert.ok(teamRetrospectiveIssues(f.root, rel).length);
+ +  data.definitionChanges = [{ role: "maker", changeRef: ".git/private.json", reasonRef: proof }]; save(); assert.ok(teamRetrospectiveIssues(f.root, rel).length);
+ +  symlinkSync(join(f.dir, proof), join(f.dir, "linked.json")); data.definitionChanges = [{ role: "maker", changeRef: "linked.json", reasonRef: proof }]; save(); assert.ok(teamRetrospectiveIssues(f.root, rel).length);
+ +});

+ +test("도달은 답만·실패 뒤 성공·실패만을 요청별로 분리하고 범위 밖 최초 요청을 제외한다", () => {

+ +  store.record({ protocol: "pace-handoff", v: 1, id: "answer-only", kind: "decision_reply", from: "owner", to: "coordinator", requestId: "answered", data: { re: "q-answered", decision: "approve", proposalHash: proposalDigest(store.read().find((r) => r.id === "q-answered")!), sourceRef: "fixture:answer-only" } }, "SYNTHETIC answer");

+ +  prepareDelivery(store, "q-retry", "retry-next", route); deliveryReceipt(store, "retry-next", { phase: "sent", reportedBy: "coordinator", sourceRef: route.sourceRef, responseHash: digest("synthetic receipt"), responseId: "fixture-success" });
+ +  const r = f.run("project", "stats", "P001", "--json"); assert.equal(r.status, 0, r.stderr); const t = JSON.parse(r.stdout).team;

+ +  const end = new Date(Date.now() + 1000).toISOString(), after = new Date(Date.now() + 500).toISOString();
+ +  const scoped = collectHandoffs(f.root, "P001", rel, { start: after, end })!; assert.equal(scoped.requests.total, 0); assert.equal(scoped.records, 0);

+ +test("역할 선언은 실제 시간/출력이나 미사용 증명이 아니며 자체 답도 명시 근거만 센다", () => {
+ +  const f = fixture(); createTeamMember(f.root, "maker", "maker"); receipt(f.dir, "proof.json", undefined, "own-answer");
+ +  const ts = new Date().toISOString();
+ +  recordEvent(f.dir, { ts, p: "P001", type: "question", by: "agent", to: "coordinator", requestId: "own-answer", msg: "합성 질문" });
+ +  recordEvent(f.dir, { ts, p: "P001", type: "answer", by: "coordinator", requestId: "own-answer", msg: "합성 코디네이터 답" });
+ +  const get = () => { const r = f.run("project", "stats", "P001", "--json"); assert.equal(r.status, 0, r.stderr); return JSON.parse(r.stdout).team; };
+ +  const old = get(); assert.equal(old.requests.coordinatorOwnAnswerObserved, null); assert.equal(old.requests.reached, 0); assert.equal(old.roles[0].workMs, null); assert.equal(old.unusedRoleCandidates[0].coverage, "unconfirmed");
+ +  const coverage = { status: "complete", reportedBy: "agent:maker", verifiedBy: "coordinator", confirmationRef: "proof.json", refs: ["proof.json"] };
+ +  const period = { start: new Date(Date.parse(ts) - 1000).toISOString(), end: new Date(Date.parse(ts) + 1).toISOString(), unit: "representative-task", closed: false, completionRef: "proof.json" }; receipt(f.dir, "proof.json", period, "own-answer"); roleReceipt(f.dir, "scope.json", period, "maker", "activity"); period.completionRef = "scope.json";
+ +  writeFileSync(join(f.dir, "TEAM-MEASUREMENT.json"), JSON.stringify({ version: 1, period, coordinator_answers: [{ requestId: "own-answer", coverage }, { requestId: "own-answer", coverage }], role_observations: [{ role: "maker", workMs: 123, outputTokens: [{ tool: "Codex", output: 42 }], coverage: { ...coverage, confirmationRef: "scope.json", refs: ["scope.json"] } }] }));
+ +  const updated = get(); assert.equal(updated.requests.coordinatorOwnAnswerObserved, 1); assert.equal(updated.requests.coordinatorOwnAnswerTotal, null); assert.equal(updated.requests.coordinatorIntendedRequests, 1); assert.equal(updated.roles[0].workMs, 123); assert.equal(updated.roles[0].source, "reported-role-binding"); assert.equal(updated.workerContext.attribution, "cwd-window-proxy-not-role");
+ +});

+ +function mergeFixture(check = "true", security = "true", measurement = true) {
+ +  const f = fixture(check, security); const started = f.run("project", "start", "P001", "--agent", "codex", "--no-launch"); assert.equal(started.status, 0, started.stderr);
+ +  const worktree = f.git("worktree", "list", "--porcelain").stdout.split("\n").filter((s) => s.startsWith("worktree ")).map((s) => s.slice(9)).find((s) => s !== f.root)!;
+ +  const run = (...args: string[]) => runPace(args, { cwd: worktree, home: f.home });

+ +  const path = join(dir, "PROJECT.md"); writeFileSync(path, readFileSync(path, "utf8").replace(/\| P-1 \| <!--.*?--> \| <!--.*?--> \|/, "| P-1 | 합성 병합 측정 | 시험 |"));

+ +  writeFileSync(join(dir, "TEAM-RETROSPECTIVE.json"), JSON.stringify({ version: 1, metricRef: "METRICS.md", unusedRoles: [], recurringFindings: [], definitionChanges: [], noChangesReasonRef: "proof.json" }));
+ +  assert.equal(f.git("-C", worktree, "add", "-A").status, 0); assert.equal(f.git("-C", worktree, "commit", "-qm", "synthetic-merge-window").status, 0);

+ +for (const removeWorktree of [false, true]) test(`project-start-to-merge 선언 입력은 실제 merge 뒤 지표를 생성한다 (worktree 제거=${removeWorktree})`, () => {
+ +  const { f, worktree, run, dir } = mergeFixture();
+ +  const finish = run("project", "finish", "P001"); assert.equal(finish.status, 0, finish.stdout + finish.stderr);
+ +  const sealed = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(dir, "TEAM-FINISH.json"), "utf8")); assert.equal(sealed.kind, "pace-team-finish");
+ +  const finishedHead = f.git("-C", worktree, "rev-parse", "HEAD").stdout.trim();
+ +  const retrospective = readFileSync(join(dir, "TEAM-RETROSPECTIVE.json"));
+ +  unlinkSync(join(dir, "TEAM-RETROSPECTIVE.json"));
+ +  assert.equal(f.git("-C", worktree, "add", "-A").status, 0); assert.equal(f.git("-C", worktree, "commit", "-qm", "synthetic-after-finish-tamper").status, 0);
+ +  const mainBefore = f.git("rev-parse", "HEAD").stdout;
+ +  const rejected = f.run("project", "merge", "P001"); assert.notEqual(rejected.status, 0); assert.match(rejected.stdout + rejected.stderr, /finish 뒤 내용/); assert.equal(f.git("rev-parse", "HEAD").stdout, mainBefore);
+ +  // Restore only this synthetic branch; real project worktrees are never reset.
+ +  assert.equal(f.git("-C", worktree, "reset", "--hard", finishedHead).status, 0); assert.deepEqual(readFileSync(join(dir, "TEAM-RETROSPECTIVE.json")), retrospective);
+ +  if (removeWorktree) {
+ +    assert.equal(f.git("worktree", "remove", worktree).status, 0);
+ +    const status = f.git("status", "--porcelain").stdout;
+ +    assert.equal(f.git("update-ref", "refs/heads/project/P001", mainBefore.trim()).status, 0);
+ +    const sameRef = f.run("project", "merge", "P001", "--yes", "--commit", finishedHead); assert.notEqual(sameRef.status, 0);
+ +    assert.equal(f.git("status", "--porcelain").stdout, status); assert.equal(f.git("rev-parse", "HEAD").stdout, mainBefore);
+ +    assert.equal(f.git("update-ref", "refs/heads/project/P001", finishedHead).status, 0);
+ +  }
+ +  const unbound = f.run("project", "merge", "P001", "--yes"); assert.notEqual(unbound.status, 0); assert.match(unbound.stdout + unbound.stderr, /승인한 SHA/);
+ +  const stale = f.run("project", "merge", "P001", "--yes", "--commit", mainBefore.trim()); assert.notEqual(stale.status, 0);
+ +  assert.equal(f.git("rev-parse", "HEAD").stdout, mainBefore);
+ +  const preview = f.run("project", "merge", "P001"); assert.equal(preview.status, 0, preview.stderr); assert.match(preview.stdout, new RegExp(`--yes --commit ${finishedHead}`));
+ +  const merge = f.run("project", "merge", "P001", "--yes", "--commit", finishedHead); assert.equal(merge.status, 0, merge.stdout + merge.stderr);
+ +  const after = JSON.parse(f.run("project", "stats", "P001", "--json").stdout); assert.equal(after.team.period.closed, true); assert.equal(after.team.period.end, after.project.merge);
+ +});

+ +test("R27 같은 시도 실패만 완료를 취소하고 다른 시도·태스크·프로젝트·legacy 기록은 보존한다", () => {
+ +  const f = fixture(), path = join(f.dir, "PROJECT.md"); writeFileSync(path, readFileSync(path, "utf8").replace(/- 상태: .*/, "- 상태: 병합 대기"));

+ +  const a = `finish:${"a".repeat(24)}`, b = `finish:${"b".repeat(24)}`, one = "2026-01-01T00:00:00Z", two = "2026-01-02T00:00:00Z", three = "2026-01-03T00:00:00Z";
+ +  type CaseEvent = { ts: string; ok: boolean; src?: string; project?: string; task?: string };
+ +  const get = (events: CaseEvent[]) => {
+ +    writeFileSync(join(f.dir, "log.jsonl"), "");
+ +    for (const e of events) recordMetric(f.dir, { project: "P001", cmd: "finish", ...e });
+ +    const r = f.run("project", "stats", "P001", "--json"); assert.equal(r.status, 0, r.stderr); return JSON.parse(r.stdout).project;
+ +  };
+ +  const success = { ts: one, ok: true, src: a }, failed = { ts: three, ok: false, src: a };
+ +  let r = get([success, failed]); assert.equal(r.finish, null, "Git 마감 메시지 근사로 취소된 성공을 되살리지 않는다"); assert.equal(r.gateFailures, 1);
+ +  assert.equal(get([success, { ts: two, ok: true, src: b }, failed]).finish, two);
+ +  assert.equal(get([success, { ...failed, src: b }]).finish, one);
+ +  assert.equal(get([{ ts: one, ok: true }, { ts: two, ok: true, src: a }, failed]).finish, one);
+ +  assert.equal(get([{ ts: one, ok: true }, { ts: three, ok: false }]).finish, one);
+ +  assert.equal(get([success, { ...failed, task: "T01" }]).finish, one);
+ +  assert.equal(get([success, { ...failed, project: "P002" }]).finish, one);
+ +});

+ +for (const mainDirect of [false, true]) test(`R27 입력 변경 실패는 동시 로그·다른 성공을 보존하고 자기 시도만 취소한다 (main=${mainDirect})`, () => {

+ +  const log = join(dir, "log.jsonl"), injected = { ts: new Date().toISOString(), p: "P001", type: "gate", by: "cli", msg: "check: checks", ok: false, data: { check: 1 }, src: "fixture:concurrent" };
+ +  const preload = join(tempDir("pace-finish-injection-"), "inject.mjs");
+ +  writeFileSync(preload, `import cp from 'node:child_process'; import {appendFileSync} from 'node:fs'; import {syncBuiltinESMExports} from 'node:module'; const original=cp.spawnSync; let fired=false; cp.spawnSync=function(command,args,options){if(!fired && command==='git' && args.includes('add') && args.includes(${JSON.stringify(join(dir, "METRICS.md"))})){fired=true;appendFileSync(${JSON.stringify(log)},${JSON.stringify(JSON.stringify(injected) + "\n")});}return original(command,args,options)};syncBuiltinESMExports();`);

+ +  assert.notEqual(finish.status, 0); assert.match(finish.stdout + finish.stderr, /검증한 입력과 고정 Git tree/);
+ +  assert.equal(f.git("-C", root, "rev-parse", "HEAD").stdout, head);
+ +  const events = readFileSync(log, "utf8").trim().split("\n").map((line) => JSON.parse(line));

+ +  assert.ok(events.some((e) => e.type === "gate" && !e.ok && e.data?.finish === 1 && e.src === own.src));
+ +  const result = runPace(["project", "stats", "P001", "--json"], { cwd: root, home: f.home }); assert.equal(result.status, 0, result.stderr);
+ +  const stats = JSON.parse(result.stdout); assert.equal(stats.project.finish, previous); assert.equal(stats.project.gateFailures, 3);

+ +test("R27 커밋 뒤 tree 불일치도 로그와 생성 커밋을 보존하고 성공 완료만 취소한다", () => {

+ +test("R27 검증 내용 지문은 나중 작업 파일/인덱스 대신 고정 tree와 대조한다", () => {
+ +  const f = fixture(), path = join(f.dir, "proof.json"); writeFileSync(path, "synthetic-before\r\n");
+ +  assert.equal(f.git("add", "-A").status, 0); const tree = f.git("write-tree").stdout.trim();
+ +  const snapshot = new InputSnapshot(); snapshot.record(path, inputHash(readFileSync(path)));
+ +  const adapter = (args: string[], env: Record<string, string> = {}) => spawnSync("git", ["-C", f.root, ...args], { encoding: "utf8", env: { ...sessionFreeEnv(), ...env } });
+ +  writeFileSync(path, "synthetic-after\n"); assert.equal(f.git("add", "-A").status, 0);
+ +  assert.doesNotThrow(() => assertTeamFinishInputs(f.root, snapshot, adapter, tree));

+ +test("R27 사용자 답은 최초 요청뿐 아니라 직접 참조한 중계 제안 뒤여야 한다", () => {
+ +  const f = fixture(), rel = `projects/${f.dir.split("/").at(-1)}`, store = new HandoffStore(f.root, "P001", rel), requestId = "relay-clock";
+ +  store.record({ protocol: "pace-handoff", v: 1, id: "root-clock", kind: "decision_request", from: "coordinator", to: "owner", requestId, data: { interpretation: false } }, "SYNTHETIC proposal");
+ +  store.record({ protocol: "pace-handoff", v: 1, id: "relay-clock", kind: "decision_request", from: "coordinator", to: "owner", requestId, data: { interpretation: false, relayOf: "root-clock" } }, "SYNTHETIC proposal");
+ +  store.record({ protocol: "pace-handoff", v: 1, id: "reply-clock", kind: "decision_reply", from: "owner", to: "coordinator", requestId, data: { re: "relay-clock", decision: "approve", proposalHash: proposalDigest(store.read()[1]!), sourceRef: "fixture:clock-answer" } }, "SYNTHETIC answer");
+ +  const rows = store.read(); rows[0]!.at = "2026-01-02T10:00:00Z"; rows[1]!.at = "2026-01-02T12:00:00Z"; rows[2]!.at = "2026-01-02T11:00:00Z";
+ +  writeFileSync(join(f.dir, "clock.json"), JSON.stringify({ version: 1, kind: "team-evidence", purposes: ["owner-answer"], sourceRef: "fixture:clock-answer", hash: digest("synthetic"), requestId, decisionReplyId: "reply-clock" }));
+ +  recordEvent(f.dir, { ts: "2026-01-02T11:30:00Z", p: "P001", type: "answer", by: "user", requestId, ref: "clock.json", msg: "synthetic time contradiction" });

+ +    const rows = store.read();
+ +    for (const row of rows.filter((r) => r.requestId === requestId)) row.at = new Date(low + (row.kind === "decision_request" ? 1 : row.data.phase === "started" ? 3 : 4)).toISOString();

"no-team" : "team", definition: "reach-v1", unit: period.unit, workType: "feature", sizeBand: "small", host: "codex", recordingBoundary: "closed-task", qualitySource: "event-log", qualityScopeHash: digest("synthetic-invariant-quality") } };

+ +test("R17 팀 사건의 문자열 배열/객체 강제 변환과 다른 스킴의 파일 경로를 거부한다", () => {
+ +  const f = fixture(), rel = `projects/${f.dir.split("/").at(-1)}`; adoptTeamMetrics(f.root, rel);
+ +  const event = { ts: "2026-01-01T00:00:00Z", p: "P001", t: "T01", type: "question", by: "agent", to: "owner", requestId: "same", msg: "synthetic question" };
+ +  for (const key of ["ts", "p", "t", "type", "by", "to", "requestId", "msg"]) {
+ +    writeFileSync(join(f.dir, "log.jsonl"), `${JSON.stringify(event)}\n${JSON.stringify({ ...event, [key]: [event[key as keyof typeof event]] })}\n`);

+ +    writeFileSync(join(f.dir, "TEAM-MEASUREMENT.json"), JSON.stringify({ version: 1, comparison: { ...snapshot().comparison, conditionCode: changed ? "team" : "no-team", sampleCount: 1 }, period, request_coverage: c, log_coverage: c, counts: Object.fromEntries(MANUAL_KEYS.slice(0, 4).map((key) => [key, 0])), coverage: Object.fromEntries(MANUAL_KEYS.slice(0, 4).map((key) => [key, c])), quality_audit: { blocking_sum: 0, rereviews_after_fail: 0, coverage: c } }));

+ +});

+ +  const f = fixture(), rel = `projects/${f.dir.split("/").at(-1)}`; adoptTeamMetrics(f.root, rel); receipt(f.dir);
+ +  writeFileSync(join(f.dir, "TEAM-RETROSPECTIVE.json"), JSON.stringify({ version: 1, metricRef: "METRICS.md", unusedRoles: [], recurringFindings: [], definitionChanges: [], noChangesReasonRef: "proof.json" }));

+ +    for (const body of [`## 참고\n\n문장${separator}## 회고\n\n${table}`, `## 회고${separator}later text\n\n${table}`, `## 회고${separator}\n\n${table}`]) { writeFileSync(join(f.dir, "PROJECT-CLOSE.md"), body); assert.ok(teamRetrospectiveIssues(f.root, rel).length, body); }

+ +  }
+ +  for (const filler of ["\u3164", "\u115f", "\u1160", "\uffa0", "\u2800"]) for (const body of [table.replace("합성", filler), table.replace("[회고]", `[${filler}]`), table.replace("구분", filler)]) { writeFileSync(join(f.dir, "PROJECT-CLOSE.md"), `## 회고\n\n${body}`); assert.ok(teamRetrospectiveIssues(f.root, rel).length); }
+ +  writeFileSync(join(f.dir, "PROJECT-CLOSE.md"), `## 회고\n\n${table}`); assert.deepEqual(teamRetrospectiveIssues(f.root, rel), []);
+ +});

+ +test("R21 단독 CR과 CRLF/LF의 실제 줄 경계를 같게 읽고 원본은 보존한다", () => {
+ +  const f = fixture(), rel = `projects/${f.dir.split("/").at(-1)}`; adoptTeamMetrics(f.root, rel); receipt(f.dir);
+ +  writeFileSync(join(f.dir, "TEAM-RETROSPECTIVE.json"), JSON.stringify({ version: 1, metricRef: "METRICS.md", unusedRoles: [], recurringFindings: [], definitionChanges: [], noChangesReasonRef: "proof.json" }));
+ +  const table = "| 구분 | 입력 | 요약 |\n|---|---|---|\n| 팀 | [회고](TEAM-RETROSPECTIVE.json) | 합성 |\n", path = join(f.dir, "PROJECT-CLOSE.md");
+ +  for (const newline of ["\r", "\r\n", "\n"]) {
+ +    const wrong = `## 회고\n\n없음${newline}## 참고\n\n${table}`; writeFileSync(path, wrong); assert.ok(teamRetrospectiveIssues(f.root, rel).length); assert.equal(readFileSync(path, "utf8"), wrong);
+ +    const good = (`## 회고\n\n${table}`).replace(/\n/g, newline); writeFileSync(path, good); assert.deepEqual(teamRetrospectiveIssues(f.root, rel), []); assert.equal(readFileSync(path, "utf8"), good);
+ +    writeFileSync(path, (`~~~md\n## 회고\n\n${table}~~~\n`).replace(/\n/g, newline)); assert.ok(teamRetrospectiveIssues(f.root, rel).length);
+ +  }
+ +});

+ +test("R22 실제 링크 이름을 제목/목적지/이스케이프 예문과 구별한다", () => {
+ +  const f = fixture(), rel = `projects/${f.dir.split("/").at(-1)}`; adoptTeamMetrics(f.root, rel); receipt(f.dir);
+ +  writeFileSync(join(f.dir, "TEAM-RETROSPECTIVE.json"), JSON.stringify({ version: 1, metricRef: "METRICS.md", unusedRoles: [], recurringFindings: [], definitionChanges: [], noChangesReasonRef: "proof.json" }));
+ +  const table = "| 구분 | 입력 | 요약 |\n|---|---|---|\n| 팀 | [회고](TEAM-RETROSPECTIVE.json) | 합성 |\n";

+ +});
